# 399: Chapter 400: My Power Switched Sides!?

The sky darkened. Six massive space rings stood aligned in mid-air.

Wind howled as Unbound Hoopa lifted its six thick severed arms beneath the rings, waving them like some demonic god.

The sheer pressure was suffocating—so heavy that it made it hard to breathe, with hearts pounding wildly.

"Dylan! This is bad! That thing's about to call for backup!" Hoopa tugged nervously at Dylan's sleeve. That wicked consciousness was completely unhinged.

Even it never dared summon six legends at once. But now? That thing was just going for it?

And Hoopa knew full well—summoned legendaries might not always play nice. They could just turn around and attack you.

Still, panic surged.

"Don't panic. Let's see who it calls." Dylan narrowed his eyes, the corners of his mouth curving into a wide grin.

He was curious to see what Unbound Hoopa had in store.

"What's that Hoopa doing? Summoning legends?" Lillie stared into the distance, watching Unbound Hoopa and the six glowing space rings in awe.

She'd seen Hoopa summon Yveltal's cocoon with a ring before, but this?

Six rings activated at once—to summon six legendaries!?

That was insane!

"And it didn't even need to possess Hoopa to form a body—that's just wild." Ilene frowned, struggling to understand.

Yveltal glanced uneasily between Unbound Hoopa and the smaller Hoopa. What even was happening here?

On the ground, the berserk Zygarde flicked its tail, its violent eyes locked on Unbound Hoopa. Even in its unstable state, instinct screamed danger.

Then, Unbound Hoopa's flailing arms stopped. The space rings' glow surged to blinding levels.

It's happening.

Dylan's eyes narrowed sharply.

"Ugh~"

From the first ring emerged a massive fish-like Pokémon, its blue body glowing with divine radiance as it unfurled its wings—

Primal Kyogre, ruler of the seas!

"Zedou~!"

The second ring unleashed a black-and-green Zygarde in its 50% form, crashing to the earth with a heavy thud.

Z1-core Zygarde, in serpentine form.

Dylan raised an eyebrow at the sight. It wasn't the flightless version?

"Oh? So now we're calling the big boys, huh? What, couldn't summon the grounded one?"

Disrespect.

From the third ring echoed a deep, draconic roar as a Pokémon cloaked in pink light floated out—

Palkia, the dragon of space!

The fourth ring glowed—Dialga, the time dragon, emerged majestically.

From the fifth ring slithered a centipede-like shadow, body rippling like smoke—

Giratina, in its Origin Forme.

And then—

From the sixth and final ring came a piercing roar as a sleek, black dragon burst into the sky.

A pitch-black serpent coiled through the air—

Shiny Rayquaza, ruler of the skies!

Six legendary Pokémon of immense stature and varied forms now filled the heavens—roaring, screeching, howling.

A scene like something from legend.

At their center stood Unbound Hoopa, crimson light glowing in its eyes, staring directly at the real Hoopa.

"This guy…"

Hoopa visibly shrank, huddling deeper into Dylan's arms.

"Why are Rayquaza and the rest just standing there behind that thing like bodyguards?" it asked, flustered.

As Hoopa itself, it knew how its powers worked.

Its space rings were ridiculously powerful—but even they couldn't force summoned legendaries to obey.

More often than not, it summoned them and immediately had to fight them.

But this?

This wasn't right.

Black Rayquaza and the others were just… standing quietly?

"Their minds are being influenced by that shadow," Dylan said, eyes scanning the six legendaries before locking on Rayquaza.

Oh boy.

The main character's ace in the movie… had switched sides.

"That's not right…" Hoopa murmured, stunned.

But now it saw it too.

Their eyes.

Just like Unbound Hoopa's—glowing with that same eerie red light.

It confirmed it.

But also… how the heck was that thing stronger than it!?

Back near the canyon's edge, Leaf had finished flattening Lysandre and the Team Flare crew, leaving Blastoise and Iron Valiant to guard them.

She returned to Lillie and the others—only to freeze.

"HUH!?"

One, two, three, four, five, six—

Six legendary Pokémon!

Add Unbound Hoopa, and that was seven monsters of legend.

Holy crap.

Ding~ Palkia, Water/Dragon-type, Spatial Pokémon

Ability: Pressure

Signature Moves: Spacial Rend, Hydro Pump, Earth Power, Aqua Tail, Power Gem, Aura Sphere…

Description: Possesses the ability to warp space. In Sinnoh's mythology, it is revered as a god. Legends from Hisui say that its birth erased the boundaries between dimensions, creating infinite space.

Palkia—the counterpart to Dialga, master of space.

"Sounds really strong~!" Lillie gasped.

Then she looked at the strange centipede-like one beside Palkia—Giratina. She didn't recognize that one.

Ding~ Giratina (Origin Forme), Ghost/Dragon-type, Renegade Pokémon

Ability: Levitate

Signature Moves: Shadow Sneak, Destiny Bond, Earth Power, Dragon Claw, Aura Sphere, Shadow Claw…

Description: Banished for its violence, it dwells in the Distortion World, silently observing the original world.

Giratina? Lillie tilted her head. That one was new to her.

"…Can't believe it's come to this," Baraz muttered, watching Dylan anxiously.

He never expected Hoopa's resentment to be so powerful. It had formed a body without needing the original.

And now it was summoning and controlling legendaries?

Too terrifying.

"Don't worry, Dylan's got this," Lillie smiled gently, reassuring the siblings.

Yeah, the enemy side was stacked. But her brother was unbeatable!

"…"

Even Yveltal, feeling the crushing pressure from Unbound Hoopa's lineup, began to hesitate.

Maybe it should just... leave?

It didn't want to get caught up in that.

But then—

Around the berserk Zygarde, space rings appeared, surrounding it completely.

The mutated Zygarde hissed, its compound eyes flicking around. It didn't understand what these rings were, but it knew it was in danger.

"…So it's targeting Zygarde first," Dylan muttered, narrowing his eyes.

"HEYY!!"

Unbound Hoopa hovered over the trapped Zygarde, flashing a terrifying grin.

Six dark fists launched from the rings—

Hyperspace Fury!

BOOM!

Each fist smashed into the injured Zygarde.

Already weakened from fighting Yveltal, the berserk Zygarde screeched in pain.

Unbound Hoopa was ridiculously powerful.

And then, the other red-eyed Zygarde slithered toward the berserk one.

"…So it's planning a fusion," Dylan confirmed.

That Zygarde lunged at its weakened counterpart, green light exploding as they merged.

"Zedou~!"

A massive roar shook the sky as emerald light formed a swirling vortex.

With a deafening blast, a colossal figure appeared in the air—

Zygarde Complete Forme.

Yes—now it could fly.

Ding~ Zygarde (Complete Forme), Dragon/Ground-type, Order Pokémon

Ability: Power Construct (Swarm Change)

Signature Moves: Core Enforcer, Thousand Waves, Thousand Arrows, Land's Wrath, Dragon Pulse, Earthquake…

Description: The complete form of Zygarde. Fires an ultimate energy blast from the mouth on its chest. (Complete Forme)

So strong!

The group stared up at the towering giant, Rotom's voice echoing in their ears.

Zygarde's full form radiated way more pressure than its 50% form. It was absurdly powerful.

And the worst part? It was now part of the enemy lineup.

Its glowing red eyes matched Unbound Hoopa's exactly.

"Holy crap, Dylan! What do we do? Even Complete Zygarde's out now!" Hoopa panicked.

It felt like a total weakling right now.

Did that crazy Hoopa really need to go this far!?

"Look at you, such a coward." Dylan flicked Hoopa's cheek, eyes shining with excitement.

Hoopa pouted.

Even back when it had full power, it wouldn't dare take on that kind of lineup alone!

Now, Unbound Hoopa let out a furious roar of malice.

"Vanish!"

Hearing its shout, Hoopa instinctively flinched—

But then puffed out its chest.

"You should vanish! Give me back my power!"

It shouted back in its childish voice, glaring up at its evil counterpart.

Unbound Hoopa's eyes flared violently, and it howled in rage.

At its command, the six legendaries moved.

"Rrrooooaaar!"

Primal Kyogre led the charge, its body glowing with primal blue light. A deep, ancient call echoed as the sky above darkened.

Primordial Sea activated!

Under its influence, the arid desert was suddenly drenched in a torrential downpour.

A ridiculous amount of rain.

Shiny Rayquaza soared into the sky, light flashing as it Mega Evolved.

Dylan's eyes lit up.

"Alright, boys—time to brawl!"

He pulled out several luxury balls and hurled them skyward.

Today's battle?

A legend in the making.

(End of Chapter)

---

# 400: Chapter 401: Are You Serious!?

"Dylan, need my help? Hoopa can still summon three buddies too!" Hoopa clung to Dylan's coat, eyes wide with nerves. Though it wasn't exactly sure the ones it summoned would help them.

"No need, Hoopa. Just stay here and watch with me," Dylan replied calmly, turning down the offer.

Seven legends as opponents—definitely tricky.

But that's what made it fun.

"Let's brawl!"

Dylan tossed several Luxury Balls into the sky.

"Linuo~!"

"Linuoma~!"

First to emerge were Dusk Mane Necrozma and Dawn Wings Necrozma.

This time, the fusions were reversed—Necrozma fused with Solgaleo, and Shiny Necrozma had taken Lunala as its partner.

"Roar!"

Kyurem appeared with a flash of electric light and immediately shifted into its Black Kyurem form.

Terapagos entered in Terastal form, and then immediately shifted into its Stellar Form, radiant with crystal energy.

Eternatus floated high above them all, massive and imposing. Its shimmering purple pupils gazed coldly over the field.

Calyrex, mounted on Spectrier, stood calmly beside Reshiram, tilting its head slightly toward the chaos ahead.

And finally, Dark Red Rayquaza landed by Dylan's side. Its noble eyes scanned Hoopa's summoned legends—

And froze on one in particular.

"…Wait. Dylan. WHY IS MY MOM ON THE OTHER SIDE?!"

Rayquaza nearly choked.

That was its mom. No doubt.

"Oh, yeah. That's Shiny Rayquaza—Hoopa summoned her over. Looks like her mind got taken over," Dylan replied casually.

Then, with a smirk, he teased, "Hey, here's your chance, Rayquaza. You've always wanted to prove yourself, right? This is your moment."

"DYLAN, YOU CAN'T BE SERIOUS! I'LL GET MURDERED! SHE'LL KILL ME!!"

Rayquaza panicked.

Compared to its strict dad, it always knew its soft-spoken mother—Shiny Rayquaza—was the real boss at home.

It had watched her beat the scales off its dad once. Nearly to death.

From that day on, Rayquaza knew: she was the real monster.

"No way, no way! Dylan, you're my trainer, my mentor! Please don't send me to my death!!"

Rayquaza shook its head frantically.

"Pathetic." Black Kyurem scoffed.

Rayquaza… bristled. Briefly.

A low, deep roar echoed through the rainclouds. Primal Kyogre loomed above, forming four orbs of gleaming blue water.

Origin Pulse!

"Stellar Cluster!"

Dylan immediately gave the command to Terapagos.

Terapagos' body glowed brilliantly as it shifted into Stellar Form. Atop its shell, the crystal pulsed.

From it launched dozens of star-like energy bursts—Stellar Cluster, Terapagos' signature move!

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Origin Pulse and Stellar Cluster clashed in a dazzling explosion.

Terapagos flew toward Kyogre—it had fought it once before in the Hoenn seas.

Dylan trusted it—Stellar Terapagos was strong enough to take on Primal Kyogre.

Twin draconic roars cut through the rain.

Dialga tilted its head back, a blue light blazing from its maw.

Palkia shifted, the pearl on its shoulder pulsing with pink light.

Roar of Time!

Spacial Rend!

Almost in unison, the two dragons attacked.

"Necrozma!"

Dylan called out.

Dusk Mane Necrozma charged through the air, burning like a solar comet.

Sunsteel Strike!

BOOM!

The strike collided with Roar of Time, pushing against the wave of temporal energy.

Dialga's eyes flickered—red light surging. It let out another roar and its energy flared—

Roar of Time exploded outward.

Dusk Mane Necrozma was forced back but stabilized itself quickly.

"Linuo!!"

It roared in frustration—clearly upset at the setback—and rushed forward again.

Dawn Wings Necrozma had blocked Palkia's Spacial Rend with Moongeist Beam, and now launched toward it in retaliation.

"Four left," Dylan muttered.

Hoopa, Shiny Rayquaza, Complete Zygarde, and Origin Forme Giratina.

Kyogre, Dialga, and Palkia were powerful—but these four were on another level.

"Vanish!"

Hoopa's furious scream echoed through the rain.

And just like that, three of them moved.

Shiny Rayquaza soared upward, its pitch-black body glowing with green light as it performed Dragon Dance.

Giratina's form shimmered in and out, weaving through the air toward Dylan's team.

"Calyrex!"

Dylan focused on the Ghost-Dragon behemoth.

Banished to the Reverse World for its cruelty—this was the God of Antimatter, stronger than most legends.

Sure, it got clowned in one movie by some guy with a gadget.

But in Hoopa's movie? Giratina was terrifying.

Shiny Rayquaza had fought six legends at once. It even blocked their first coordinated attack with Delta Stream Tornado, powered up by dual Psychic boosts.

And when it hit Giratina with Dragon Ascent?

Nothing.

Literally nothing.

Giratina tanked the hit, got pushed into a wall, and casually shot back a Shadow Ball.

So yeah. Giratina?

Serious business.

"Giratina, huh~"

Calyrex raised a hand. Its sharp, intelligent eyes locked onto the ghostly dragon.

Spectrier shrieked, galloping skyward.

Black spirit particles swirled into hundreds of tiny orbs.

Astral Barrage!

Calyrex brought down its arm, sending a wave of black spirits at Giratina.

Giratina's glowing red eyes scanned the attack. Its body turned semi-transparent—

BOOM! BOOM!

The barrage struck what seemed like Giratina's body, exploding in shadowy blasts.

But from within the blast zone, Giratina's phantasmal form slipped out—turning solid mid-flight as it lunged at Calyrex.

Shadow Force.

Dylan's eyes gleamed.

Unlike Shadow Sneak or Phantom Force, Giratina's Shadow Force wasn't just a "disappear and reappear" move. Its real body entered another dimension, leaving only a projection behind.

The attacks had struck the afterimage—but the real Giratina was elsewhere.

"Spectrier!"

Calyrex called, and its steed dashed back, opening distance again.

Calyrex was strong and fast—enough to last even against Giratina.

That left…

"Zygarde's 4x weak to Ice. Kyurem, he's yours."

Dylan turned to Black Kyurem.

Sure, Zygarde was strong. But that weakness?

Fatal.

"Leave it to me! Dragons are my specialty!"

Black Kyurem grinned, energy swirling at its tail. Glacial-blue energy surged around it as it dove straight for Zygarde.

"Eternatus—Eternamax!"

Eternatus vanished into the clouds. Dark vortices spun above, glowing with sinister red light.

Its massive claw burst through the stormclouds, clashing with Shiny Rayquaza mid-air.

Two top-tier dragons unleashed overwhelming pressure.

"Rayquaza, guard us. If Hoopa tries anything funny, jump in."

Dylan said to his crimson Rayquaza beside him.

He still had Dialga and Shiny Mewtwo in reserve—but Mewtwo was Psychic-type. It'd be heavily countered by Hoopa.

"Got it, Dylan—WAIT! SHE'S COMING STRAIGHT FOR ME!!!"

Rayquaza's confident voice cracked as a flash of green light streaked toward them.

It knew that glow.

Dragon Ascent.

"Eternatus—Max Cannon!"

Dylan commanded as he patted Reshiram's side.

Reshiram lifted off to retreat—but Rayquaza's mom was fast.

Eternatus opened its massive claw and fired a huge crimson blast toward the Dragon Ascent—

But a golden ring appeared mid-air.

Swoosh—

The beam vanished into it.

"…Tch."

Dylan's brows furrowed.

Hoopa's rings could absorb energy attacks. Every one of them.

"Rayquaza! Get up there!"

"WHAT!? Alright—I—I'm going!!"

Rayquaza gritted its teeth and flew up, body glowing with green light as it used its own Dragon Ascent.

Mom… forgive me. This isn't personal!

BOOM!

The two green beams collided.

But in an instant, Rayquaza's glow shattered.

Shiny Rayquaza's attack cut straight through, blasting Rayquaza mid-air.

Shiny Rayquaza turned, red eyes glowing coldly as it looked down at the falling Rayquaza.

Yep.

Rayquaza got one-shot.

Total crash landing.
